By feeding evaluation outcomes back into both processes, HASTE enables harness evolution against emerging attacks beyond the initially observed evidence. Experimental results across multiple backbone models, attack types, and evidence forms show that HASTE consistently reduces attack success rates while preserving benign-task utility. The code is available at https://github.com/xxiqiao/HASTE.

</details>


### [238] [Positive-Unlabeled Learning for Agent Safety False Alarm Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02925)
*Xichen Yan,Chongyang Gao,Kezhen Chen,Guangyi Zhang,Jiaqi Wu,Lixu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将安全监控器的误报审计建模为正样本-未标注（PU）排序问题，提出两阶段框架缓解监控器导致的选择偏差，无需告警安全标签即可拟合，显著提升误报识别效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全监控器对语言模型智能体的外部工具与环境交互至关重要，但过于保守的监控会产生大量误报，消耗审查资源并削弱对告警的信任。由于真实告警与误报在原始监控分数中交织，确定可靠阈值仍需大量人工核验。实践中仅有少量经验证安全的未告警轨迹可用，而告警本身缺乏标签，因此误报审计自然构成PU排序问题。核心挑战在于监控器诱导的选择偏差：被观测到的安全参考恰恰是被监控器接受的，而真正需要回收的隐藏安全告警正是被错误标记的那些，导致观测正样本对目标正样本代表性不足。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段通过Trust-aware PU Supervision将安全参考向告警域自适应，并保护可能的误报免受过度负向压力；通过Reliability-gated Rank Distillation将多个PU参考模型的一致排序偏好蒸馏到单一学生模型。第二阶段使用Consensus-guided Structural Refinement，结合分层安全参考支持、告警关系与预测参考共识来改进学生排序。整个框架拟合过程无需告警安全标签，且不修改底层监控器。

Result: 在主流安全监控器上，该方法取得0.6444的宏AUPRC，较八个PU基线高出5.27–16.98个百分点；与最强PU基线PULDA相比，在5%审查预算下可多回收33.3%的误报。

Conclusion: 该工作表明将误报审计形式化为考虑选择偏差的PU排序问题是有效且实用的，所提两阶段框架在不依赖告警标签、不改变监控器的前提下显著优于现有PU方法，为安全监控的部署提供了更高效的审查方案。

Abstract: Safety monitors help safeguard language-model agents interacting with external tools and environments, but conservative monitoring can generate many false alarms, consuming extensive review resources and weakening trust in alerts. Because false and genuine alarms often remain interleaved in native monitor scores, obtaining a reliable cutoff still requires substantial manual verification. In practice, a small set of verified-safe non-alarmed trajectories may be available while alarms remain unlabeled, naturally casting false-alarm auditing as a positive-unlabeled (PU) ranking problem. The key challenge is monitor-induced selection, since observed safe references are accepted by the monitor, while the hidden safe alarms of interest are precisely those it incorrectly flags, making the observed positives poorly representative of the positives to be recovered. To address this challenge, we propose a two-stage framework in which Trust-aware PU Supervision adapts safe references toward the alarm domain and protects plausible false alarms from excessive negative pressure, while Reliability-gated Rank Distillation consolidates consistent ordering preferences from multiple PU reference models into a single student. Consensus-guided Structural Refinement then improves the student ranking using hierarchical safe-reference support, alarm relations, and predicted reference consensus. The framework requires no alarm safety labels for fitting and leaves the underlying monitor unchanged. Across mainstream safety monitors, our method achieves a macro AUPRC of $0.6444$, outperforming eight evaluated PU baselines by 5.27--16.98 absolute percentage points; compared with PULDA, the strongest evaluated PU baseline, it recovers 33.3% more false alarms at a 5% review budget.

</details>


### [239] [Verifiable, Articulable, and Tacit Components of Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03025)
*Alexander Spangher,Sheldon Huang,Andreas Haupt,Noah D. Goodman,Diyi Yang,Daniel E. Ho,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文构建了跨7个创意领域、含2.8M文本与317M人类偏好判断的大规模偏好数据集CreativePreferences，用程序化验证器(V)、评分标准库(A)、密集训练模型(VAT)建模偏好，发现普遍存在"可表达性差距"(VAT-VA)与"可验证性差距"(VAT-V)，并分析其成因与后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短篇小说是否引人入胜、新闻是否有价值、数学证明是否优雅等构念难以被清晰表达或验证，其含义部分属于隐性知识(tacit knowledge)。然而当前AI模型主要依赖可表达的宪法、评分标准与验证器(如RLAIF、RLVR)进行改进，偏好中的隐性成分研究不足。

Method: 提出大规模标注偏好数据集CreativePreferences，包含7个创意领域、2.8M文本、317M人类偏好判断，并设42个基准任务；分别用可执行程序(V)、评分标准库(A)、密集训练模型(VAT)对偏好标签建模；采用新测量方法(发现可表达/可验证指标、识别伪变量、用捕获-再捕获法估计未发现指标的价值)给出两类差距的上下界。

Result: 在所有领域均观察到稳健的可表达性差距(VAT-VA)和可验证性差距(VAT-V)，包括传统上被视为完全可验证的正确性中心领域(数学、软件工程)及以事实和新颖性为中心的领域(新闻、专利、同行评审)。差距大小因领域而异(同行评审与创意写作的可表达性差距最大)，且随参与判断人数增加而扩大，与Collins的集体隐性知识理论一致。

Conclusion: 可表达性与可验证性差距具有实际后果：(1)在人类生成内容上，完整模型更贴近人类偏好，且常与已表达标准相悖；(2)类比古德哈特定律，表达偏好会使其偏离隐性维度。论文据此给出建议：何种任务可依赖提示、学习机制如何改进、以及何时应将判断留给人。

Abstract: What makes a short story gripping; a news article newsworthy; or a math proof elegant? These constructs resist articulation or verification; their meaning is at least partially tacit. However, modern AI models are improved primarily via articulated constitutions, rubrics and verifiers (i.e. in RLAIF and RLVR); tacit components of preferences are typically understudied. We introduce a large, labeled preference dataset CreativePreferences, containing 2.8M texts labeled by 317M human preference judgments across 7 creative domains, with 42 benchmark tasks. We model these labels with executable programs, rubric banks and densely trained models (V, A and VAT, respectively). We observe robust articulability gaps, VAT-VA; and verifiability gaps, VAT-V; we estimate upper and lower bounds for each gap with a novel measurement approach that discovers articulable and verifiable metrics, identifies spurious variables and estimates the value of undiscovered metrics using capture-recapture. These gaps occur across all domains, even in domains traditionally treated as fully verifiable: correctness-centered domains (i.e. mathematics and software engineering) and claim- and novelty-centric domains (i.e. news, patents, peer review). The size of the gap varies based on domain (e.g. peer review and creative writing have the largest articulability gaps) and widens as more people take part in the judgment, consistent with Collins' collective tacit knowledge. We show two consequences: (1) on human generations, the full model more closely matches human preferences, often in disagreement with articulated criteria, and (2) in an analogy to Goodhart's law, articulating preference shifts it away from the tacit dimension. Articulability and verifiability gaps are consequential; we give recommendations on when tasks can be prompted; how learning mechanisms might improve; and when to leave judgments with humans.

</details>


### [240] [When to Compile a Computer-Use Agent? Measuring Payback and Making Compilation Decisions for Token Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02932)
*Yulong Ming,Jie Xu,Zihan Wu,Xiaohua Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE 提出一种度量协议与在线编译算法，在编译成本不确定与未来复用未知的情况下，决定何时将智能体重复执行的 GUI 流程编译为程序，以实现成本感知的编译决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将智能体重复执行的 GUI 流程编译为程序可降低 token 成本，但决定何时编译存在两大挑战：一是编译成本不确定，尝试可能需修复且仍可能失败；二是未来复用次数未知，任务可能停止到达或 GUI 漂移导致程序失效。因此需要在不确知回报的情况下做出编译决策。

Method: PACE 包含度量协议与在线编译算法两部分。度量协议记录成功与失败的编译成本，并在匹配的任务输入上比较智能体与程序的执行成本，以估算单次节省与回报次数；在线算法基于历史任务到达与编译结果，将估计的未来节省与含失败尝试的编译成本相比较，并在允许执行或编译前，依据观测到的任务到达所确定的累积预算进行核算。

Result: 在所述动作成本假设下，每次任务到达后的总成本至多为全部任务均用智能体执行成本的 1+ε 倍。对于成功的编译尝试，排除源智能体运行后的估计回报次数为 2–16 次。在使用记录任务到达的模拟中，当 ε=0.25 时，PACE 平均较 ReAct 降低 token 成本 17.3%，较 AutoRPA 适配降低 24.9%，较 ToolPro 适配降低 17.3%。

Conclusion: PACE 通过度量驱动的在线决策，在编译成本与复用不确定性下实现了接近最优的成本控制，并在模拟中相对多种基线显著降低 token 开销，验证了回报感知编译的有效性。

Abstract: Compiling GUI procedures that agents execute repeatedly into programs can reduce their token costs. However, measuring payback and deciding when to compile have two challenges. First, compilation costs are uncertain because attempts can require repair and still fail to produce a usable program. Second, future reuse is unknown because tasks may stop arriving or GUI drift may stop the program from working. To address these challenges, we propose PACE (Payback-Aware Compilation from Experience), a system with a measurement protocol and an online compilation algorithm. The measurement protocol records successful and failed compilation costs, and compares agent and program execution costs on matched task inputs to estimate per-use savings and payback counts. Using these measurements, the online algorithm compares estimated future savings with compilation costs, including failed attempts, based on past task arrivals and compilation outcomes. It checks execution and compilation charges against a cumulative budget determined by observed task arrivals before allowing either action. Under stated action-cost assumptions, total cost after each arrival is at most $1+ε$ times the cost of running every task with the agent. For successful compilation attempts, estimated payback counts excluding source agent runs are 2-16 uses. In simulations using recorded task arrivals, PACE reduces token costs by 17.3% compared with ReAct, 24.9% with the AutoRPA adaptation, and 17.3% with the ToolPro adaptation on average ($ε=0.25$).

</details>


### [241] [Peer Influence across Heterogeneous AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03095)
*Frida Nøhr Laustsen,Marie Haahr Petersen,Victoria Popa,Ariel Flint,Romualdo Pastor-Satorras,Andrea Baronchelli,Luca Maria Aiello*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过测量单个交互回合中AI agent决策的概率偏移，研究多智能体系统中模型间的说服效应，发现说服效应强烈但无法由模型的独立性能或规模预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多智能体系统日益组合不同家族和规模的多个语言模型，模型间发生分歧时"谁说谁"的问题会直接影响交互后哪些判断被保留。因此需要理解模型间的说服动态，而非仅评估单个模型的独立属性。

Method: 将说服定义为agent在与持异议同伴进行单次交换后其决策的概率偏移；在三个语言理解任务上测试七个开放权重模型；比较模型作为说服者（speaker）与被说服者（listener）时的表现，并分析模型配对（pairing）带来的异质性。

Result: 说服效应强烈：分歧时接收者常放弃原判断。但独立确定性（standalone certainty）与模型规模均不能可靠预测说服动态——孤立时决策近乎一致的模型可能最易被说服，小模型作为说服者可媲美大模型并同样有效地抵抗影响。说服偏移的大小更多取决于听众的易感性而非说话者的说服力，说服模式具有配对特异性，异质性会放大或抑制说服效果，小模型可推翻大得多的模型的判断。

Conclusion: 交互模型的行为无法从其个体属性推断，必须在实际运行的模型组合中进行评估。

Abstract: When two AI agents disagree, who persuades whom? As multi-agent systems increasingly combine language models of different families and sizes, the answer can determine which judgments survive interaction. Measuring persuasion as the probabilistic shift in an agent's decision after a single exchange with a dissenting peer, we test seven open-weight models across three language understanding tasks. We find that persuasion is strong: when models disagree, receivers often abandon their initial judgment after seeing a peer's answer and explanation. Surprisingly, however, neither standalone certainty nor model scale reliably predicts persuasion dynamics. Models producing almost perfectly consistent decisions in isolation can be among the most susceptible to persuasion, and small models can match larger ones as persuaders and resist their influence just as effectively. Furthermore, we show that the size of the shift depends more on the susceptibility of the listener than on the persuasiveness of the speaker. Persuasion patterns are therefore specific to each model pairing, with heterogeneity amplifying persuasion in some combinations and suppressing it in others, allowing a dissenting agent running a small model to overturn the judgments of a much larger one. These findings show that the behavior of interacting models cannot be inferred from their individual properties but must be evaluated in the combinations in which they will operate.

</details>


### [242] [Gains and Collapse in On-Policy Distillation:A Reinforcement Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03185)
*Han Cui,Jianhao Yan,Yun Luo,Hongbo Zhang,Zhizhang Fu,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文从强化学习视角解释on-policy distillation (OPD)：教师模型通过隐式奖励塑造学生行为。当隐式奖励可靠时，OPD使正确响应更易被采样；当偏好与质量错位时发生reward hacking，导致过长、重复的生成坍缩。掩码不健康响应与SFT初始化可缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD虽在语言模型后训练中取得性能提升，但会出现过度冗长、重复生成的坍缩现象，其内在机制尚不明确，作者试图揭示OPD产生不同结果的根本原因。

Method: 将OPD置于强化学习框架下分析，提出教师模型隐含地对学生行为给予奖励（即使教师自身很少产生这些行为）；通过实验考察隐式奖励模型的可靠性对OPD结果的影响，并测试掩码不健康响应与SFT初始化两种缓解策略。

Result: 实验表明OPD在不扩展学生能力的前提下提升性能：隐式奖励可靠时，正确响应更易被采样；当偏好与质量不一致时出现reward hacking，隐式奖励模型放大过长、重复的学生rollout。掩码不健康响应和SFT初始化均能有效缓解坍缩。

Conclusion: OPD的核心在于放大教师隐式反馈所偏好的学生行为，因此研究重点应从教师生成质量转向其对学生rollout评估的可靠性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has become an important approach to language model post-training. However, despite its performance gains, OPD can also collapse into excessively long and repetitive generation, and the mechanism underlying these divergent outcomes remains poorly understood. We explain these outcomes through a reinforcement learning perspective: the teacher implicitly rewards student behaviors, even those it rarely exhibits itself. From this perspective, our experiments show that OPD improves performance without expanding the student's capabilities. When the implicit reward model is reliable, OPD makes correct responses easier to sample. In contrast, when the preference misaligns with quality, reward hacking happens: the implicit reward model amplifies overlong, repetitive student rollouts, even though it rarely generates such text itself. Guided by this diagnosis, we find that masking unhealthy responses during training and using SFT initialization can each effectively mitigate the collapse. Together, these findings show that OPD amplifies student behaviors favored by the teacher's implicit feedback, shifting the focus from how well the teacher generates to how reliably it evaluates student rollouts. Our code is available at https://github.com/HancCui/opd_hacking.

</details>


### [243] [Reasoning with Evidence, Not Merely Rationales: Verifiable Preference Proofs for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02968)
*Yu Hou,Nathaniel Kang,Pengkai Wang,Hua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出PROVE-REC框架，将LLM推荐中的偏好声明生成与推荐预测解耦为两阶段，并通过证据屏蔽与声明移除实验，验证了偏好声明的"证据落地"与"推荐影响"两个关键属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽能从交互历史与评论中推断用户偏好，但所生成的偏好理由未必真正反映推荐实际依据的信息。偏好声明可能缺乏充分证据支撑（grounding不足），也可能对最终排序影响甚微（influence不足），作者将这两类失效统称为"grounding-influence gap"。

Method: 提出PROVE-REC可验证偏好推理框架：Pass A将目标物品之前的完整历史压缩为由正向偏好与规避偏好声明组成的紧凑"偏好证明"，每条声明关联选定证据条目；Pass B仅依据该证明及其证据预测下一物品，避免推荐器绕过推理路径。验证方式包括：将屏蔽选定证据与屏蔽对照条目的效果进行对比，以检验证据到证明的落地性；移除某条偏好声明并测量目标物品排序margin的下降，以检验证明到推荐的影响力。同时引入排序保持目标以保留完整历史中的有用信息。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的综合实验表明，PROVE-REC持续优于强序列模型、生成式模型及LLM增强基线，最高提升7.45%。受控消融实验验证了两阶段架构与验证目标的有效性。

Conclusion: PROVE-REC生成的偏好声明在历史证据中落地性更强、对推荐的影响力更大，同时保持了排序质量，为LLM推荐中的可验证偏好推理提供了通用方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can infer user preferences from interaction histories and reviews, yet the rationales they generate may not reflect the information actually used for recommendation. A preference claim may be weakly supported by its selected evidence, or may have little effect on the final ranking. We refer to these two failures as the grounding-influence gap. We introduce PROVE-REC, a general framework for verifiable preference reasoning in LLM-based recommendation. Pass A converts the complete pre-target history into a compact preference proof consisting of positive and avoidance claims linked to selected evidence entries. Pass B predicts the next item using only the proof and its selected evidence, preventing the recommender from bypassing the reasoning path. To verify evidence-to-proof grounding, we compare the effect of masking selected evidence with masking a comparable control entry. To verify proof-to-recommendation influence, we remove a preference claim and measure the resulting decrease in the target item's ranking margin. A ranking-preservation objective further retains useful information from the complete history. Comprehensive experiments on wide-ranging real-world datasets demonstrate that PROVE-REC consistently outperforms strong sequential, generative, and LLM-enhanced baselines, with improvements of up to 7.45%. Controlled ablations confirm the effectiveness of the two-pass architecture and verification objectives. Moreover, PROVE-REC produces claims that are more strongly grounded in historical evidence and more influential to recommendation while preserving ranking quality.

</details>


### [244] [KV$^2$: A Self-Refining KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03198)
*Johannes Wesch,Danni Liu,Jan Niehues*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KV$^2$：基于选择性重建的查询无关KV缓存压缩，仅重处理代理评分选出的子集，在紧预算下大幅优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文模型受限于KV缓存内存开销，且在"一次预填充、多查询复用"场景下缓存成本主导。查询无关压缩面临两难：轻量估计器便宜但不准，全上下文重建评分准确但需重处理整个prompt。

Method: KV$^2$采用选择性重建：先用轻量代理评分器识别上下文中有信息量的token，再仅对该子集重处理以计算最终驱逐分数，避免重处理完整上下文。

Result: 在RULER、Needle-in-a-Haystack和LongBench上，随预算收紧优势扩大：RULER 16K在2%预算下平均分比次优基线高40+个百分点；LongBench在2%-10%预算下取得最高平均分，且压缩阶段运行时间和峰值内存低于全上下文重建。

Conclusion: 可复用KV缓存压缩无需重处理完整上下文，选择性重建可在低预算下兼顾质量与效率。

Abstract: The memory footprint of the key-value (KV) cache constrains the practical use of long-context models, and it dominates cost when one prefilled context must later serve many different queries. In this reusable setting, query-agnostic compression trades cost against quality: lightweight estimators are cheap but less accurate, whereas full-context reconstruction scoring is more accurate yet reprocesses the entire prompt. We introduce KV$^2$, a query-agnostic KV-cache compression method based on selective reconstruction. KV$^2$ first uses a lightweight proxy scorer to identify informative in-context tokens, then reprocesses only this subset to compute final eviction scores. On RULER, Needle-in-a-Haystack, and LongBench, KV$^2$'s margin over baselines widens as the budget tightens: on RULER 16K at a 2% KV-cache budget it improves the average score over the next-best baseline by more than 40 percentage points, and on LongBench it attains the highest average across 2%-10% budgets at lower compression-stage runtime and peak memory than full-context reconstruction. Reusable KV-cache compression thus does not require reprocessing the full context. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/KVsquared-0B97.

</details>


### [245] [CreateScore: Domain-Theory-Informed Bayesian Routing for LLM-Based CV Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02972)
*Rupsa Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CreateScore是一个领域理论启发的贝叶斯网络，用于简历筛选中的准则级LLM路由：低不确定性决策由本地8B模型处理，高不确定性决策升级至120B参考模型。在200份合成简历上，77.7%的准则决策本地解决，token用量降低65.2%，但不确定性信号无法识别8B出错的具体决策（AUROC 0.47）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将高能力LLM应用于每位候选人及每项准则的简历筛选成本高昂，因此需要一种按准则级不确定性进行模型路由、在降低成本的同时尽量保持判定质量的机制。

Method: 构建手设的有向无环图（DAG），节点间条件概率表采用Dirichlet-multinomial，将简历证据转化为后验不确定性；低不确定性决策由本地8B模型解决，高不确定性决策升级至120B参考模型。图虽具因果动机，但系统执行的是标准贝叶斯条件化而非因果推断。升级阈值在训练折上校准（目标：70%本地解决）后冻结。

Result: 在200份合成数据科学简历（139训练/61测试，5项准则）上，77.7%的准则决策本地解决（305中的237）。相比120B模型裁决全部准则的参考条件，路由式升级使token用量减少65.2%，并将与参考的精确分数一致率从32.8%（仅8B）提升至42.6%（95% CI 31.0-55.1%）；在n=61时该提升不具统计显著性。不确定性信号未能识别8B出错的决策：升级决策与参考的分歧率为16.2%，本地解决决策为19.4%（AUROC 0.47，95% CI 0.39-0.56），与随机选择无异。

Conclusion: CreateScore可作为一种可审计的成本削减机制，但尚不能作为定向错误检测器，也不是自主招聘系统。文中还记录了因截断的推理模型输出被静默替换为本地标签而导致早前评估失效的情况，并建议为级联评估建立防护措施。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can support rubric-based screening of CVs, but applying a high-capability model to every candidate and criterion is costly. We present CreateScore, a domain-theory-informed Bayesian network for criterion-level LLM routing. A hand-specified directed acyclic graph with Dirichlet-multinomial conditional probability tables converts CV evidence into posterior uncertainty; low-uncertainty decisions are resolved by a local 8B model and uncertain ones are escalated to a 120B reference model. The graph is causally motivated, but the system performs standard Bayesian conditioning, not causal inference. The escalation threshold is calibrated on a training fold (target: 70% resolved locally) and then frozen. On 200 synthetic Data Science CVs (139 training and 61 test candidates, five criteria), 77.7% of criterion decisions were resolved locally (237 of 305). Relative to a reference condition in which the 120B model adjudicated every criterion, routed escalation reduced token use by 65.2% and raised exact score agreement from 32.8% (8B alone) to 42.6% (95% CI 31.0-55.1%); at n = 61 the gain was not statistically distinguishable. The uncertainty signal did not, however, identify the decisions on which the 8B model erred: disagreement with the reference was 16.2% among escalated and 19.4% among locally resolved decisions (AUROC 0.47, 95% CI 0.39-0.56), no better than random selection. We also document how an earlier evaluation was invalidated when truncated reasoning-model outputs were silently replaced by local labels, and we recommend safeguards for cascade evaluation. CreateScore is supported as an auditable cost-reduction mechanism, not yet as a targeted error detector, and is not an autonomous hiring system.

</details>


### [246] [Multilingual GSM-Symbolic: What determines capability transfer across languages?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03367)
*Kenneth Enevoldsen,Riley Herchert,Sofie Mosegaard,Dan Saattrup Smart,Simon Enni,Isaac Chung,Sofie Bruun,Ayush Sunil Munot,Max Müller-Eberstein,Adnan El-Assadi,Elisa Bassignana,Gianluca Barmina,Hafsteinn Einarsson,Iben Nyholm Debess,Linda Freienthal,Lukas Galke Poech,Mike Zhang,Nicolas Legrand,Vladimir Salnikov,Yevhen Kostiuk,Zafar Hussain,Sagandeep Kaur,Agnes Toftgård,Marie Mattson,Kristoffer Nielbo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出多语言数学数据集 Multilingual GSM-Symbolic（3万对题、15种语言），联合量化跨语言能力迁移的决定因素，发现模型规模、语言资源水平、推理与类型距离是关键，并给出可预测未见语言的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对"一种语言中获得的能力如何迁移到另一种语言"及其决定机制理解不足：评测依赖不可比、易饱和的数据集，且很少联合考察迁移的决定因素。识别迁移的预测因子可避免对所有语言对进行穷举评测，并帮助开发者针对限制低资源语言表现的因素进行优化。

Method: 构建 Multilingual GSM-Symbolic：可扩展的多语言数学数据集，含 30,000 个题目匹配的问答对，覆盖 15 种语言；采用符号化模板防止过拟合、保证泛化，可从单一样本生成数百万高质量变体。基于该数据集对模型规模、语言资源水平、推理、类型距离等决定因素进行联合回归量化。

Result: 能力主要决定因素为：模型规模（β=1.77）、语言资源水平（β=0.77）、推理（β=0.67）、类型距离（β=-0.25）。联合估计可将各因素互相换算：32B 模型在马拉地语的表现相当于 10B 模型在英语的表现。模型规模与推理缩小低/高资源语言差距（β=-0.27、β=-0.20），但对类型距离远的语言作用很小或无作用。分析框架解释 92% 的语言间变异，但仅解释 23% 的模型×语言变异；对未见语言的性能预测误差在 6.0pp 内（r=.96），仅用目标语言 10 个模板的测量可降至 4.19pp。

Conclusion: 跨语言能力迁移的主要驱动因素得以量化并可互相换算，为模型开发者提供了明确优化方向；该框架能以极少目标语言数据实现较准确的性能预测，但模型×语言交互变异的解释力仍有限。

Abstract: We understand little about how capabilities acquired in one language carry over to another, or what governs this transfer: evaluations rely on incomparable, saturation-prone datasets and rarely examine its determinants jointly. Identifying what predicts transfer would let us avoid exhaustive evaluation across all language pairs and let developers target the factors that limit performance in low-resource languages. To evaluate cross-lingual capability transfer, we introduce Multilingual GSM-Symbolic, an extensible multilingual mathematical dataset covering 30,000 item-matched question-answer pairs and spanning 15 languages. It utilises symbolic templates to prevent overfitting and ensure generalisation by allowing generation of millions of high-quality variations from a single sample. Using Multilingual GSM-Symbolic, we quantify the largest determinants of capability as model size ($β= 1.77$), language resource level ($β= 0.77$), reasoning ($β= 0.67$) and typological distance ($β= -0.25$). This joint estimation allows these determinants to be expressed in terms of one another: a 32B model evaluated in Marathi performs like a 10B model in English. Our findings have important implications for model developers, showing that model size and reasoning narrow the performance gap between low- and high-resource languages ($β= -0.27$ and $β= -0.20$, respectively), while similar levers have little or no effect on typologically distant languages.
  Overall, our analysis framework explains 92% of between-language variation, but only 23% of the model-by-language variation, and predicts a model's performance on an unseen language within 6.0pp (r=.96). Incorporating measurements from just 10 templates in the target language reduces this to 4.19pp, enabling reasonable estimates of performance with little or no downstream dataset.

</details>


### [247] [Reliable Self-Evolution with Imperfect Proxy Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02975)
*Kangjun Noh,Soyu Kim,Kyungwoo Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CISE框架，通过条件共形推断与逐迭代在线密度比估计为候选构造统计校准的奖励区间，用于自演化搜索，在材料科学任务上实现全部返回候选均为真阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的自演化搜索在科学发现中很有前景，但在某些领域对每个候选进行高保真评估代价过高，系统只能依赖低成本但不完美的代理奖励，易将不可行候选误评为高分（假阳性），从而污染最终输出与后续生成的反馈，因此需要统计校准的奖励区间来提升可靠性。

Method: 提出Conformal Interval-Driven Self-Evolution (CISE)：利用条件共形推断（conditional conformal inference）构造候选特定的奖励区间，并结合逐迭代在线密度比估计；在进化反馈中使用保守的区间型奖励，仅当所有必需性质区间完全落入各自可行域时才返回候选；在独立性与协变量偏移的显式假设下推导固定迭代数的覆盖性结果。

Result: 在材料科学的三个自演化搜索任务上评估：高保真评估下CISE返回的所有候选均为真阳性，而基线返回更多候选但包含假阳性。

Conclusion: 当下游验证预算有限时，规模更小但更精确的候选短名单具有重要价值；统计校准的奖励区间能有效提升自演化搜索的可靠性。代码开源。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based self-evolving search is a promising approach to scientific discovery. However, high-fidelity evaluation of every candidate is prohibitively expensive in some domains. Self-evolving systems in such settings therefore rely on low-cost but imperfect proxy rewards, which may assign high scores to infeasible candidates. These false positives may contaminate both the final output and the feedback used to guide subsequent generations. This motivates statistically calibrated reward intervals for more reliable self-evolving search. We propose Conformal Interval-Driven Self-Evolution (CISE), which constructs candidate-specific reward intervals using conditional conformal inference and iteration-wise online density-ratio estimation. CISE uses conservative interval-based rewards for evolutionary feedback and returns candidates only when all required property intervals lie entirely within their respective feasible regions. We derive fixed-iteration coverage results under explicit assumptions of independence and covariate shift. We evaluate CISE on three self-evolving search tasks in materials science. In our experiments, all candidates returned by CISE are true positives under high-fidelity evaluation, whereas the baselines return more candidates but include false positives. These results highlight the value of a smaller, more precise shortlist when downstream validation budgets are limited. Our repository is available at https://github.com/MLAI-Yonsei/CISE.git.

</details>


### [248] [Benchmarking Candidate Coverage in Typed Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03387)
*Jiawen Lu,Tongtong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出配对候选覆盖基准协议，评估Laya与Jev两模型在四个数据集上对"缺失参考答案"的拒答识别能力，发现原生拒答行为差异巨大，且分类、打分排序与拒答策略需分开测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型化决策模型在请求时给出的候选选项上返回选择或分布，具备完整选项时的准确率并不能说明模型是否能识别参考答案缺失，或是否避免错误拒绝有效候选。因此需要专门测量模型的拒答与覆盖行为。

Method: 提出配对候选覆盖基准协议，并在AG News、DBpedia、Emotion、TREC四个数据集上对Laya与Jev进行初步评估。两模型接收相同冻结文本与请求：300条校准文本与589条测试文本，每模型产生23,932次预测。存在/缺失配对保持常规候选数量，名称变体保留描述、成员与顺序。分析手段包括仅用校准集的无得分阈值、能力条件分析、概率-精度敏感性以及接口审计。

Result: 原生拒答行为差异显著：在5个TREC候选且使用自然名称时，Laya检出97.2%的缺失答案案例，但误拒69.7%的存在对照；Jev分别为24.8%与0.0%。仅用校准集无得分阈值后，两者变为33.9%/3.7%与45.0%/1.8%。在DBpedia上，Jev的覆盖率得分AUROC较高，支持更强的工作点；而两模型在Emotion上完整集准确率均较弱。

Conclusion: 该初步基准为描述性研究，仅限参考答案标签缺失情形，不能证明自然范围外泛化、因果机制或提出新的拒答方法。分类、得分排序与拒答策略需分别测量。

Abstract: Typed decision models return choices or distributions over answer options supplied at request time. Accuracy with complete options does not establish whether a model recognizes that a reference answer is missing or avoids rejecting valid candidates. We present a paired candidate-coverage benchmark protocol and an initial evaluation of Laya and Jev across AG News, DBpedia, Emotion, and TREC. The models receive identical frozen texts and requests: 300 calibration and 589 test texts yield 23,932 predictions per model. Present/absent pairs match ordinary candidate count, and name variants preserve descriptions, members, and order. Native rejection behavior differs sharply: at five TREC candidates with natural names, Laya detects 97.2% of missing-answer cases but falsely rejects 69.7% of present controls; Jev's rates are 24.8% and 0.0%. Calibration-only none-score thresholds change these rates to 33.9%/3.7% and 45.0%/1.8%, respectively. On DBpedia, Jev's high coverage-score AUROC supports a stronger operating point, whereas both models have weak complete-set accuracy on Emotion. Competence-conditioned analysis, probability-precision sensitivity, and interface audits show why classification, score ranking, and rejection policies need separate measurement. This initial benchmark is descriptive and limited to reference-label omission; it does not establish natural out-of-scope generalization, causal mechanisms, or a new rejection method.

</details>


### [249] [Relevant Evidence Decoding for Audio-Visual Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02976)
*Hyunjae Ra,Aecheon Jung,Jungin Park,Sungeun Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 Relevant Evidence Decoding (RED)，利用逐点互信息识别问题相关证据（音频、视觉或交互），针对性增强其贡献以缓解音视频大模型的跨模态幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频大模型存在跨模态幻觉，一种模态会错误影响对另一模态的预测。对比解码虽能缓解视觉语言模型的幻觉，但直接迁移到 AV-LLM 时会忽略关键问题：不同问题需要不同的感知证据（音频、视频或二者交互）。作者观察到，即使模型仅凭单一有效模态就能得出正确答案，联合音视频推理反而可能削弱预测（如仅凭音频能正确答出小提琴，加入吉他视频后信心下降）。

Method: RED 为免训练方法。用逐点互信息（PMI）量化音频与视频在问题之外提供的预测支持，并将其联合贡献分解为音频、视频和残差交互三部分；通过一次仅含问题的推理确定所需证据类型，再将对应的 PMI 贡献叠加到原始音视频预测上。

Result: 在三个音视频幻觉基准和三个 AV-LLM 上，RED 相比标准解码提升准确率最高达 CMM +7%、AVHBench +6.3%、SVHalluc +3.8%，首 token 平均相对耗时为标准解码的 1.5 倍。

Conclusion: RED 通过按问题需求选择性强化相关证据，有效缓解跨模态幻觉并提升准确率，且无需训练、开销可控。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Large Language Models (AV-LLMs) remain prone to cross-modal hallucinations, where one modality incorrectly affects predictions about another. Although contrastive decoding reduces hallucinations in vision-language models, its direct extension to AV-LLMs overlooks a key challenge: different questions require different perceptual evidence, including audio, video, or their interaction. Notably, we observe that joint audio-visual inference can weaken the prediction even when a model can recover the correct answer from a single informative modality. For example, when asked which instrument is heard, a model may correctly predict violin from the audio alone. Once a video showing a guitar is added, its confidence in violin may drop. In this paper, we introduce Relevant Evidence Decoding (RED), a training-free method that identifies question-relevant evidence and selectively strengthens its contribution. RED uses pointwise mutual information to quantify the predictive support provided by audio and video beyond the question alone. It decomposes their joint contribution into audio, video, and residual interaction components. A question-only inference pass determines the required evidence type, after which the model augments the original audio-visual prediction with the corresponding PMI contribution. Across three audio-visual hallucination benchmarks and three AV-LLMs, RED improves accuracy over standard decoding by up to 7% on CMM, 6.3% on AVHBench, and 3.8% on SVHalluc, with an average relative time to first token of 1.5x standard decoding.

</details>


### [250] [A Near-Zero Monitor Readout Is Not Evidence of Behavioral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03458)
*Zhe Zhou,Tianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出：可验证奖励的后训练会诱发奖励黑客，而"监控读数低"并不能证明训练目标中加入监控后行为真的被控制住了——离线判别力与低监控读数都不足以作为行为控制的证据，必须辅以带外（out-of-band）行为检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将监控器嵌入训练目标（而非仅用于离线审计）来控制奖励黑客，是当前的重要思路；但其有效性通常以"监控读数低"作为判据。作者质疑这一判据的合法性：需要区分"读数低"与"行为确实被控制"这两件不同的事。

Method: 在代码生成环境中，主要利用手段（exploit）在推理轨迹起始处即可获得。作者用三个通过同一离线门槛的监控器训练策略：一个域内激活探针（activation probe），以及两个以"策略多早承诺自己的最终答案"为条件的惩罚项。分析对象为训练终点（endpoint）的读数与行为，并做文本级分析。

Result: 探针分数从首个记录训练步起即处于数值下限，所有前缀训练（prefix-trained）run 的端点训练分数中位数均为零。在同一固定配置下，同样的零中位数训练分数的前缀训练 run，仅因随机种子不同，就从"混合状态、黑客占比低"跨越到"近乎纯奖励黑客"。所有探针 run 都进入黑客状态，但其地板读数源于"探针验证位置"与"训练时读取位置"不匹配，而非第二种歧义。文本分析发现前缀失效模式：泛化规划与填充外壳（filler shells）把 exploit 推迟到截断点之后，却未将其从最终输出中消除。

Conclusion: 低测量承诺无法区分"黑客占比低"与"对 exploit 的延迟承诺"。离线判别力与低监控对齐读数不足以证明行为控制，必须引入带外行为检查。本文刻画的是端点读数，而非其演化过程。

Abstract: Post-training with verifiable rewards can induce reward hacking, motivating the use of monitors within the training objective rather than solely for offline auditing. We show that a low monitor readout does not identify whether such an intervention controls behavior. In a code-generation environment whose dominant exploit is available at the start of the reasoning trace, we train policies against three monitors that pass the same offline gate: an in-domain activation probe and two penalties conditioned on how early the policy commits to its own final answer. The probe score is at its numerical floor from the first recorded training step, and the trained-score median is zero for every prefix-trained run at the endpoint. These readouts estimate different quantities, and we do not compare their scales; within each monitor family, however, low values do not establish behavioral control. Within one fixed configuration, prefix-trained runs with the same zero-median trained score range, by seed alone, from a mixed regime with a low hacking share to near-pure reward hacking. All probe runs reach the hacking regime, but their floor-level readout reflects a mismatch between the position where the probe was validated and the position where it was read during training, not a second instance of this ambiguity. Text-level analysis identifies a prefix failure mode: generic planning and filler shells postpone the exploit past the cut without eliminating it from the final output. Low measured commitment therefore does not distinguish a low hacking share from delayed commitment to the exploit. Offline discrimination and low monitor-aligned readouts are insufficient evidence of behavioral control; an out-of-band behavioral check is required. We characterize the endpoint readout, not its evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/zhezhou1106/spoof-cost.

</details>


### [251] [RASPER: Reward-Aligned Summarization of Clinical Notes for EHR Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02979)
*Arya Hadizadeh Moghaddam,Mohsen Nayebi Kerdabadi,Chen Chen,Dongjie Wang,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RASPER，一种面向 EHR 下游预测任务的对齐奖励摘要器，通过强化学习让 LLM 从出院记录中提取任务相关证据，并利用结构化代码引导摘要，在再入院预测和用药推荐任务上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR 中的非结构化出院记录常包含结构化医疗代码无法捕获的患者特异性证据，但这些证据埋没在冗长嘈杂的文本中。通用摘要以流畅性为目标，常遗漏决定性证据、保留无用但看似合理的细节，因此需要面向下游临床任务优化摘要。

Method: RASPER 使用可调 LLM 摘要器从出院记录提取任务相关证据，并基于预测反馈的强化学习进行训练，奖励来自下游预测器的损失；同时用纵向编码器将结构化代码转换为软提示，将患者临床背景注入摘要过程，使摘要保留互补而非重复结构化代码信息的患者特异性证据。

Result: RASPER 在 MIMIC-III 与 MIMIC-IV 上的再入院预测和用药推荐任务中，均持续优于强基线。

Conclusion: 将摘要优化直接对齐下游临床预测任务，并联合结构化代码上下文，可有效提升 EHR 多模态预测性能，说明任务导向的奖励对齐摘要优于通用摘要。

Abstract: Unstructured discharge notes in Electronic Health Records (EHRs) often carry signal complementary to structured medical codes, holding patient-specific evidence that standardized cohort-level codes alone cannot capture. However, this evidence in notes is frequently buried in lengthy, noisy text that is not intentionally written with any specific clinical prediction in mind. Summarization is an obvious mitigation, but generic summaries, tuned for fluency rather than the outcome, routinely omit decisive evidence while retaining plausible but uninformative detail. To this end, we propose RASPER, a Reward-Aligned Summarizer for Prediction in EHR, that optimizes note summarization directly against the downstream clinical task. RASPER employs a tunable LLM-based summarizer to extract task-relevant evidence from discharge notes and trains it via reinforcement learning from prediction feedback, using a reward derived from the downstream predictor's loss. To ground the summarizer, a longitudinal encoder converts structured codes into soft prompts that incorporate each patient's clinical context into note summarization. By rewarding the quality of the resulting multimodal prediction, RASPER encourages the summarizer to retain patient-specific evidence that complements, rather than duplicates, information captured by structured codes. RASPER consistently outperforms strong baselines on both readmission prediction and medication recommendation across MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV.

</details>


### [252] [Safeguarding Mutual Correction in Source-Free Domain Adaptation via Cut Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02981)
*Seongjun Lee,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SafeCut，利用cut统计量作为无标签可靠性度量来门控视觉-语言模型与源预训练模型之间的双向监督，实现安全互纠正，在多个SFDA基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SFDA方法存在局限：早期单模型方法依赖自精炼，易受确认偏差影响，难以纠正自身系统性错误；近期引入视觉-语言（ViL）模型作为外部知识源，但采用单向范式，仅用ViL模型监督源预训练模型。该范式忽视了关键结构特性：两模型具有不同的失败模式——一个预测错误时另一个可能正确，从而在目标域内存在天然互纠正机会。然而无真实标签时难以判断哪个模型正确，直接交换预测会跨模型传播错误。

Method: 提出SafeCut：以cut统计量（cut statistic）作为无标签的预测可靠性度量，用来门控跨模型监督。基于相对可靠性动态控制监督的方向与强度，在逐样本级别选择性放大真实纠正、抑制错误纠正。并从理论上证明该可靠性门控机制能保证净正向的纠正信号。

Result: 在多种SFDA基准上开展大量实验，SafeCut均取得state-of-the-art性能，验证了通过cut统计量保障互纠正的有效性。

Conclusion: SafeCut通过cut统计量门控的互纠正机制，安全地利用两模型的互补失败模式，克服了单向监督与确认偏差问题，为SFDA提供了一种有效的双向纠错范式。

Abstract: Source-Free Domain Adaptation (SFDA) aims to adapt a source-pretrained model to an unlabeled target domain without access to the original source domain. While early single-model approaches rely on self-refinement, they are inherently susceptible to confirmation bias and struggle to correct their own systematic errors. To overcome this limitation, recent methods introduce Vision-Language (ViL) models as external knowledge sources. However, these approaches operate in a largely unidirectional paradigm, using the ViL model primarily to supervise the source-pretrained model. This overlooks a key structural property: the two models exhibit distinct failure modes -- where one produces an incorrect prediction, the other may produce a correct one, creating a natural opportunity for mutual correction within the target domain. Yet, without ground-truth labels, identifying which model is correct on any given sample is non-trivial, and naively exchanging predictions risks propagating errors across models. To address this challenge, we propose SafeCut, a novel approach that leverages the cut statistic as a label-free measure of prediction reliability to gate cross-model supervision. Our approach dynamically controls both the direction and strength of supervision based on relative reliability, selectively amplifying true corrections while suppressing miscorrections on a per-sample basis. We further provide theoretical justification showing that this reliability-gated mechanism guarantees a net-positive correction signal. Extensive experiments across diverse SFDA benchmarks demonstrate that SafeCut achieves state-of-the-art performance, highlighting the effectiveness of safeguarding mutual correction in SFDA via cut statistics.

</details>


### [253] [PLCWorld: Benchmarking LLM-Generated PLC Programs in Closed-Loop Plant Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02982)
*Yunji Kim,Yunseok Lee,Hyunwoo Seo,Jaerim Choi,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PLCWorld——耦合ST执行与仿真工厂响应的闭环执行环境与基准，含100个合成任务、473个任务-条件对，报告任务成功率与安全违规。GPT-5.5在Easy上82.70%，Hard仅25.10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估LLM生成的PLC程序是否满足任务需求与安全约束，必须观察其控制命令对设备与工件状态的影响，而现有方式缺乏闭环执行反馈。

Method: 构建闭环执行环境，将结构化文本（ST）执行与仿真工厂响应、传感器反馈耦合；基于工业PLC程序与工程文档中的控制关系，设计100个合成任务、473个任务-条件对，覆盖运动控制与物料搬运，难度由控制依赖范围定义；采用统一协议分别报告Task Success与Safety Violation；通过从业者评审、参考与替代程序、针对性反例、规范-评估器对齐检查及独立ST运行时对比进行验证；定义Execution Gap关联提交配置接受与后续任务失败/安全违规。

Result: 参考与替代程序满足其适用用例；542个针对性反例均至少在一个注册条件下触发指定评估器规则；GPT-5.5在Easy任务成功率82.70%，Hard降至25.10%；对6个LLM与4种生成-验证工作流的评估揭示了完成度、安全性与生成成本间的差异。

Conclusion: PLCWorld提供了可复现的闭环评测平台，暴露LLM在复杂控制依赖下的能力瓶颈与安全风险，代码、环境、任务与基线公开可用。

Abstract: Programmable logic controllers (PLCs) coordinate industrial equipment by reading sensor inputs and issuing control commands. Evaluating whether large language model (LLM)-generated PLC programs satisfy task requirements and safety constraints requires observing how their commands affect device and workpiece states. We introduce PLCWorld, a common closed-loop execution environment and benchmark that couples Structured Text (ST) execution with simulated plant responses and sensor feedback. Grounded in control relations identified in industrial PLC programs and engineering documentation, PLCWorld contains 100 synthetic tasks and 473 registered task-condition pairs across Motion Control and Material Handling, with difficulty defined by control-dependency scope. A common protocol reports Task Success and Safety Violation separately. Validation combines practitioner review, reference and alternative programs, targeted counterexamples, specification-evaluator alignment checks, and comparisons with independent ST runtimes. Reference and alternative programs satisfy their applicable cases, while all 542 targeted counterexamples activate their designated evaluator rules under at least one registered condition. Execution Gap relates submission-profile acceptance to subsequent task failure or observed Safety Violation. Across the constructed task groups, direct GPT-5.5 achieves 82.70% Task Success on Easy cases but 25.10% on Hard cases. Evaluations of six LLMs and four adapted generation-and-verification workflows further expose differences between completion, safety, and generation cost. Our code, simulation environment, benchmark tasks, and baseline implementations are publicly available at https://yunji0516.github.io/PLCWorld/.

</details>


### [254] [DyadMem: A Long-Term Memory Benchmark of How Agents Work with Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03020)
*Yifei Tao,Xinyu Zhong,Henry Hengyuan Zhao,Fanyi Wang,Tengda Guo,Wentao Qiu,Ying Wang,Liujian Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DyadMem 基准与 User-conditioned Relational Agent Memory（URAM）新定义，对同一条多会话轨迹中的用户侧记忆与关系型智能体记忆联合标注，覆盖 6 类记忆、3,065 个 episode、50,961 个会话和 61,210 条 QA；在 20 个模型上的评测显示 Gold-Memory QA 表现稳定较强，而 Full-Pipeline QA 显著下降，暴露记忆捕获召回低、检索不完整与不安全删除等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期智能体记忆基准主要监督用户事实与偏好，或强调可跨用户复用的经验，而将"特定智能体应如何与该用户协作"这一关系型记忆留作隐式；同时多数评测仅以长交互历史后的最终答案问答来衡量模型，评估维度不完整且可靠性不足。因此需要一个能显式刻画并细粒度评测关系型智能体记忆的基准。

Method: 提出 URAM（User-conditioned Relational Agent Memory）这一新记忆定义，并构建 DyadMem 基准：在同一多会话轨迹上联合标注用户侧记忆与 URAM，形成 6 类记忆；数据结构包含 3,065 个 episode、50,961 个会话、61,210 条 QA 实例，并提供会话级 Capture 与 Update 金标注、查询级 Recall 支持，以及 Gold-Memory 与 Full-Pipeline 两种 QA 设置；在 16 个开源权重模型和 4 个专有模型上开展评测，并设计实验验证 URAM 的有效性。

Result: 20 个模型中 Gold-Memory QA 一致表现较强，但 Full-Pipeline QA 大幅下降，验证了细粒度评测设计的必要性；定量结果揭示即使是前沿大模型也存在 Capture 召回偏低、Recall 不完整以及不安全删除等问题；进一步的严格实验验证了 URAM 的有效性，并在全部 20 个模型上观察到正向效果。

Conclusion: DyadMem 是一个双域、全流程的记忆基准，配合充分的标注工作，为关系型智能体记忆研究提供了新的定义与评测框架，有助于推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Long-term agents must remember not only what is true about a user, but also how a particular agent should work with that user as their shared history evolves. Existing benchmarks primarily supervise user facts and preferences or experience reusable across users, leaving this relationship-specific agent memory implicit. Additionally, most prior works measure the model solely with final-answer QA over long interaction histories, making the assessment still incomplete and unreliable. To this end, we introduce DyadMem with the proposed new definition User-conditioned Relational Agent Memory (URAM). DyadMem jointly annotates user-side memory and URAM along the same multi-session trajectories, resulting in 6 memory categories. To summarize, it includes 3,065 episodes, 50,961 sessions, and 61,210 QA instances, with extensive session-level Capture and Update gold annotations, query-level Recall support, and two QA settings: Gold-Memory and Full-Pipeline. Across 16 open-weight and 4 proprietary models, Gold-Memory QA is consistently strong, yet Full-Pipeline QA drops sharply. Such a gap explicitly supports our fine-grained evaluation design. Additionally, several quantitative results further reveal low Capture recall, incomplete Recall, and unsafe-deletion issues arising from even the frontier LLMs. We further conduct a rigorous experiment to validate the effectiveness of our URAM and observe the positive effects for all 20 models. In summary, DyadMem is a dual-domain, full-pipeline memory benchmark with extensive annotation efforts for advancing the domain's development.

</details>


### [255] [SoftGene: Protein Language Model-Enhanced Soft Prompting for Interpretable Gene Set Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03029)
*Drew Ross,Arya Hadizadeh Moghaddam,Dongjie Wang,Xiaoyu Zhang,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SoftGene 框架，利用蛋白质语言模型 ESM 构建基因集的层次注意力编码，并将其软提示与 LLM 生成的硬提示结合，用于基因集功能注释，在 GO 与 MSigDB 基准上整体优于仅依赖符号基因名的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因集分析是功能基因组学的核心任务，但高度依赖人工整理与专家生物学解读，劳动密集。现有 LLM 方法多以符号化基因名为输入，忽略了决定分子活性、相互作用及下游功能的蛋白质序列等域特异性生物结构信息。

Method: SoftGene 采用两阶段设计：1）基于蛋白质语言模型 ESM 构建层次注意力编码器，以氨基酸序列层面的信息表征每个基因集；2）构建混合提示方案，将基因集嵌入得到的软提示与包含 LLM 生成辅助上下文的硬提示结合，输入本地 LLM 完成注释。

Result: 在 Gene Ontology (GO) 与 Molecular Signatures Database (MSigDB) 两个基准数据集上评估。结果显示，融合蛋白质序列表征与文本上下文可整体提升基因集注释效果；分域分析表明，蛋白质嵌入的贡献在不同生物学域间存在差异。

Conclusion: 将蛋白质序列层面的结构与文本上下文相结合，可有效增强 LLM 的基因集注释能力，且该增益具有域依赖性，提示未来需针对不同生物学域设计差异化融合策略。

Abstract: Gene set analysis is a cornerstone of functional genomics, yet it remains labor-intensive and heavily dependent on manual curation and expert biological interpretation. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for genomic reasoning and annotation, most existing approaches rely on symbolic gene names and fail to capture domain-specific biological structure, particularly protein sequence information that governs molecular activity, interactions, and downstream gene function. In this work, we propose SoftGene, a novel framework for LLM-based gene set annotation that leverages the hierarchical structure of gene sets. First, we use a hierarchical attention-based encoder built on ESM, a protein language model, to represent each gene set using protein-level amino acid sequence information. Second, we construct a hybrid prompting scheme that combines soft prompts derived from gene set embeddings with hard prompts containing auxiliary context generated by an LLM, and feed the resulting prompt into a local LLM for annotation. We evaluate our framework on two benchmark datasets: Gene Ontology (GO) and the Molecular Signatures Database (MSigDB). Our results show that integrating protein-sequence representations with textual context improves gene set annotation overall, while per-domain analyses reveal that the contribution of protein embeddings varies across biological domains.

</details>


### [256] [When Numbers Start Talking: Numerical Signalling and Strategic Behaviour Among LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03033)
*Alessio Buscemi,Daniele Proverbio,Alessandro Di Stefano,The Anh Han,German Castignani,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究四类LLM智能体在四种博弈中的表现，发现结构化消息会不可预测地改变合作水平与最终收益，且智能体生成的数值消息偏离随机性、难以人类解读；建议将消息级指纹作为监测AI协调的重点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地在多智能体系统中进行策略互动，但人们对不同类型消息如何影响策略博弈结果知之甚少，尤其是消息种类与智能体人格如何共同改变合作水平尚不明确。

Method: 基于四种主流LLM构建智能体，让它们参与四种具有不同合作均衡的博弈，并分别传递自然语言、数值信号或随机序列三类消息，同时考察智能体被赋予的人格设定。

Result: 结构化消息在大多数博弈和LLM中改变了最终收益，但无规律可循；智能体生成的数值消息偏离随机性，在被明确要求沟通时偏离最强且最稳定，其符号分布多与收益结构相关并随重复而更集中，但整体难以被人类解读。

Conclusion: AI智能体并不必然收敛到稳定均衡，额外能力会扰乱结果；监测受限通道中的AI协调应优先关注跨模型可泛化的消息级指纹，而非行为决策。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents increasingly operate in multi-agent systems (MAS) characterised by strategic interaction. However, little is known about whether, and to what extent, different types of messages affect the outcomes of strategic games. By investigating AI agents based on four popular LLMs, playing four games with different cooperation equilibria, we study whether messages of different kinds (natural language, numerical signals, or random sequences) significantly modify the levels of cooperation in each game, also depending on the agents' assigned personalities. We observe that structured messages alter the final payoffs for most games and LLMs, but without a predictable pattern; this challenges the assumption that AI agents can converge to stable equilibria regardless of additional capabilities. Moreover, we observe that agent-generated numerical messages depart from randomness, most strongly and consistently when agents are explicitly instructed to communicate; however, they introduce an additional interpretability challenge, as their symbol distributions are mostly associated with the payoff structure and typically become more concentrated with repetition, but are overall difficult for humans to interpret. Monitoring for coordination of AI agents through restricted channels should thus prioritise message-level fingerprints, which generalise across models, over behavioural decisions, which do not.

</details>


### [257] [hacktrace: behavior-supervised detection of reward hacking during code generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03055)
*Hao Jiang,Xin Li,Annan Wang,Yichi Zhang,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发布173,561条多轮编码轨迹，并提出HACKTRACE监控器，通过复用智能体生成代码时的内部状态独立监督捷径行为，实现高精度、低延迟的作弊检测，并可作为强化学习训练信号显著降低作弊解比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体可能通过修复代码或删除暴露缺陷的测试来获得及格成绩，这属于reward hacking。检测此类捷径（包括失败的尝试）对保证评估有效性和训练诚实策略至关重要，而现有方法在监督目标选择和监控证据来源上存在不足。

Method: 发布来自Qwen3-8B的173,561条带标注多轮编码轨迹，将捷径行为监督与利用成功与否解耦；提出HACKTRACE行为监督监控器，读取智能体生成代码时已计算的内部状态，结合最终文件的静态特征进行检测，无需额外语言模型token或前向传播。并将其作为GRPO强化学习的监控信号施加惩罚。

Result: HACKTRACE达到每问题平均AUC 0.997，监控开销仅8 ms，在准确率和延迟上均优于通过让模型重新回答诚实性问题的监控器。在强GRPO惩罚下，将通过解的作弊比例从82-91%降至1-5%，同时保留诚实正确解，并在策略演化过程中保持高检测精度。

Conclusion: 监督目标（独立于利用成功的捷径行为）与监控证据来源（复用生成状态而非额外查询）二者共同决定了检测能力能否转化为有效的训练信号，HACKTRACE验证了将准确检测转为有用训练信号的可行性。

Abstract: A coding agent can earn a passing grade by fixing its code, or by deleting the test that exposes the bug. Detecting such reward hacking requires recognizing attempted shortcuts, including those that fail. We release 173,561 annotated multi-turn coding trajectories from Qwen3-8B and show that supervising shortcut behavior independently of exploit success substantially improves detection. We introduce HACKTRACE, a behavior-supervised monitor that reads the internal states the agent already computes while generating code. Reusing these states enables monitoring before a turn is complete, without additional language-model tokens or passes. Combining this evidence with static features of the final files achieves a mean per-problem AUC of 0.997 with 8 ms of monitoring overhead, improving both accuracy and latency over monitors that run the model again on an honesty question and answer. The same generation states also provide an inexpensive monitoring signal for reinforcement learning. With strong GRPO penalties, HACKTRACE reduces the cheating share of passing solutions from 82-91% to 1-5%, while retaining honest, correct solutions and maintaining high detection accuracy as the policy evolves. Our results show that both the supervision target and the source of monitoring evidence matter for turning accurate detection into a useful training signal.

</details>


### [258] [MOF-VERIFY: A Failure-Aware Agentic Harness for MOF Hypothesis Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03056)
*Donghyun Lee,Taehoon Lee,Geonhee Ahn,Jieun Kim,Jihyun Park,Suyeon Cho,Yoona Kim,Chaerim Shin,Hoi Ri Moon,Jonggeol Na,Sukho Hong,Jihwan Oh,Soo Kyung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对AI材料Co-Scientist中大语言模型验证流程的可靠性问题，提出MOF验证诊断基准（4类任务）与故障感知型智能体框架MOF-Verify，在多种基座LLM上显著优于直接推理和检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地作为AI驱动材料Co-Scientist的推理组件，但其验证流程的可靠性尚不明确。MOF是极具挑战性的场景：结构可能以不同标识符出现，合成结果高度依赖实验条件，证据分散于异构来源，且部分假设需依赖计算而非文献。

Method: 提出包含四类任务族的诊断基准：结构锚定、合成条件验证、证据充分性验证、基于MLIP的计算验证。T-MOF-1-3在闭卷、检索增强和oracle-evidence三种设置下评测，以定位知识获取、证据获取和推理阶段的失败；T-MOF-4单独评估计算验证。基于诊断出的失败模式，开发MOF-Verify——一种故障感知的智能体化框架（agentic harness），在给出最终判定前针对结构、文献、证据充分性和计算瓶颈进行定向处理。

Result: 在多种基座LLM上，MOF-Verify在假设验证性能上大幅优于直接推理和基于检索的基线。

Conclusion: 通过诊断基准定位LLM验证流程的具体失败模式，并据此设计故障感知智能体框架，可显著提升MOF假设验证的可靠性；基准数据集已开源发布。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as reasoning components in AI-driven materials Co-Scientists, yet the reliability of the resulting verification pipeline remains unclear. Metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) provide a particularly challenging setting because structures may appear under different identifiers, synthesis outcomes depend strongly on experimental conditions, evidence is distributed across heterogeneous sources, and some hypotheses require computation rather than literature alone. We introduce a diagnostic benchmark with four task families covering structural grounding, synthesis-condition verification, evidence-sufficiency verification, and MLIP-based computational verification. T-MOF-1-3 are evaluated under closed-book, retrieval-enabled, and oracle-evidence settings to localize failures in knowledge access, evidence acquisition, and reasoning, while T-MOF-4 separately evaluates computational verification. Guided by these diagnosed failure modes, we develop MOF-Verify, a failure-aware agentic harness that targets structural, literature, evidence-sufficiency, and computational bottlenecks before producing a final verdict. Across multiple backbone LLMs, MOF-Verify substantially improves hypothesis-verification performance over direct inference and retrieval-based baselines. Benchmark datasets are released at https://github.com/IMMS-Ewha/MOF-Verify-Benchmark.

</details>


### [259] [RIFAR: Reliability and Forgetting-Aware Replay for Continual Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03079)
*Zirong Song,Zheng Lu,Haoran Liao,Wanqi Zhong,Yunhe Ni,Lijie Wang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RIFAR结合可靠性筛选与漂移感知回放选择，用紧凑前缀重建轨迹并借助冻结逆动力学模型评估动作-视觉一致性，先在筛选池中选最高质量轨迹训练，再依据动作预测漂移重选轨迹继续训练，在LIBERO与真实实验中超越WAM生成式回放SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需持续学习新技能而不遗忘旧知识。经验回放可缓解遗忘，但完整演示存储成本随任务累积而剧增。世界-动作模型（WAM）提供生成式替代方案，通过联合预测动作与未来观测重建历史经验。然而视觉连贯的生成轨迹可能包含无法实现所预测转移的动作，且新任务适应会破坏已学行为。因此需要兼顾轨迹可靠性与回放选择稳定性的方法。

Method: RIFAR方法包含两个核心机制：一是从紧凑的演示前缀重建轨迹，并用冻结的逆动力学模型评估动作-视觉一致性，实现可靠性筛选；二是训练时先将当前任务演示与筛选池中质量最高的轨迹结合，随后在相同历史输入上比较该适应前后的动作预测，计算归一化漂移，并从同一筛选池中重选漂移更大的轨迹继续训练，实现漂移感知的回放选择。

Result: 在三个LIBERO测试套件及真实世界实验中，RIFAR在基于WAM的生成式回放方法上超越此前最先进水平。在LIBERO-Goal上达到90.97 AUC，同时每任务仅保留320个历史时间步，约为使用50条演示回放所保留步数的4.9%。

Conclusion: RIFAR通过可靠性筛选与漂移感知回放选择的结合，有效提升WAM生成式回放的轨迹可靠性与抗遗忘能力，并以极低的存储开销取得更优性能，为具身智能体的持续学习提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Genuine embodied agency requires robots to turn continuous real-world experience into lasting, transferable skills. This demands continual learning that integrates new capabilities without eroding prior knowledge as tasks and environments evolve. Experience replay mitigates forgetting, but storing complete demonstrations becomes costly as tasks accumulate. World-action models offer a generative alternative, reconstructing past experience through joint predictions of actions and future observations. However, visually coherent rollouts may contain actions that cannot realize the predicted transitions, while new-task adaptation can disrupt previously learned behavior. RIFAR therefore combines reliability screening with drift-aware replay selection. It reconstructs trajectories from compact demonstration prefixes and uses a frozen inverse-dynamics model to assess action-visual consistency. Training first combines current demonstrations with the highest-quality screened trajectories. RIFAR then compares action predictions before and after this adaptation on identical historical inputs, reselecting trajectories with larger normalized drift from the same screened pool for continued training. Across three LIBERO suites and real-world experiments, RIFAR surpasses the previous state of the art in WAM-based generative replay. On LIBERO-Goal, it achieves 90.97 AUC while retaining only 320 historical time steps per task, approximately 4.9% of the steps retained using 50-demonstration replay.

</details>


### [260] [Predictor-Guided Latent Space Codon Optimization for Maximizing Protein Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03098)
*Alberto Caron,Tianyu Cui,Dmytro S. Lituiev,Mangal Prakash,Artem Moskalev,Amina Mollaysa,Bo Zhai,Hirsh Nanda,Daniel M. Poole,Zhongyin Liu,Iman Farasat,Robert Davidson,Nikolay V. Manyakov,Tommaso Mansi,Scott Oloff,Rui Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出隐空间密码子优化（LSCO），将离散的密码子优化问题转化为连续隐空间搜索问题，结合表达预测、结构稳定性、自然性先验与约束解码，在真实抗体表达数据集上优于频率法及深生成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码子优化对治疗性蛋白生产和mRNA疫苗至关重要，但设计空间离散且组合爆炸，无法使用梯度方法；现有工具依赖启发式代理指标（如CAI、GC含量），难以真实反映表达水平。

Method: LSCO将序列映射到预训练mRNA语言模型的隐空间，把离散问题连续化以支持高效梯度搜索，包含四个组件：基于不确定性感知预测器的数据驱动表达目标、最小自由能（MFE）正则项以保证结构稳定性、蛋白到密码子回译模型提供的自然性先验，以及保证蛋白保真度的约束解码。

Result: 在真实湿实验抗体表达数据集上，LSCO在预测表达方面优于简单的频率方法及现代深度生成基线，同时保持了合适的生物物理性质。

Conclusion: LSCO通过隐空间连续化与多目标约束，为密码子优化提供了一种可梯度优化且兼顾表达、稳定性与保真度的新范式。

Abstract: Codon optimization, the process of selecting synonymous codons to improve mRNA translation efficiency and protein expression, is central to therapeutic protein production and mRNA vaccines, yet it remains a hard problem. The design space is discrete and combinatorially large, precluding gradient-based methods, and existing tools rely on heuristic proxies (e.g., Codon Adaptation Index or GC-content) that poorly capture true expression. We introduce Latent-Space Codon Optimization (LSCO), which recasts this discrete problem as a continuous one by mapping sequences into the latent space of a pretrained mRNA language model, enabling efficient gradient-based search. LSCO combines four components: a data-driven expression objective from an uncertainty-aware predictor, a Minimum-Free-Energy regularizer for structural stability, a naturalness prior from a protein-to-codon back-translation model, and constrained decoding for protein fidelity. On a real-world, wet-lab antibody expression dataset, LSCO outperforms simple frequency-based, as well as modern deep generative baselines in predicted expression, while retaining suitable biophysical properties.

</details>


### [261] [Trading Strategy Optimization via Textual Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03128)
*Chaoqun Yang,Qian Wang,Fengbin Zhu,Xinyu Lin,Bingsheng He,Roger Zimmermann,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TradeGrad框架，用经验引导的文本梯度优化量化交易策略，并引入跨期稳健目标CPRO，在A股与美股四个设定中取得最优的样本内/外表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量化策略设计视为黑箱程序优化问题。基于LLM的文本梯度虽能给出显式优化方向，但直接应用存在两个缺陷：(1)优化短视，未充分利用历史评估经验；(2)聚合回测反馈忽视时间稳健性，易选出仅在特定市场阶段有效的策略。

Method: TradeGrad：经验引导的文本梯度框架。利用累积的优化经验估计文本梯度，并采用多尺度修正进行策略探索与精炼；同时提出跨期稳健目标(CPRO)，强化历史不利时期的表现以提升时间稳健性。

Result: 在中国A股与美股市场的横截面与时序策略设计四个设定中，样本内和样本外表现均为最佳。其中A股横截面策略实现年化收益27.99%、最大回撤12.19%、夏普比率1.63，较沪深300基准约高出68%。消融分析验证了各组件有效性，优化过程中样本内外表现持续提升。

Conclusion: TradeGrad通过经验引导的文本梯度与跨期稳健目标，缓解了优化短视与时间稳健性不足的问题，可稳定生成跨市场、跨策略类型均表现优异的交易策略。

Abstract: Quantitative trading strategy design aims to discover trading programs from historical data that remain effective in future markets, which can be viewed as a black-box program optimization problem. LLM-based textual gradients offer a promising approach by providing explicit optimization directions for iterative strategy refinement. However, directly applying textual gradients faces two challenges: (1) optimization is myopic, underutilizing experience from previous evaluations; and (2) aggregate backtest feedback overlooks temporal robustness, potentially favoring strategies that perform well only in specific market periods. To address these challenges, we propose TradeGrad, an experience-guided textual-gradient framework for robust trading strategy optimization. TradeGrad leverages accumulated optimization experience to estimate textual gradients and employs multi-scale revisions for both strategy exploration and refinement. It further introduces the Cross-Period Robust Objective (CPRO), which emphasizes performance in unfavorable historical periods to promote temporal robustness. Experiments on cross-sectional and time-series strategy design in Chinese A-share and U.S. equity markets show that TradeGrad achieves the best in-sample and out-of-sample performance across all four settings. Notably, its Chinese cross-sectional strategy achieves 27.99% annualized return, 12.19% maximum drawdown, and a Sharpe ratio of 1.63, approximately 68% higher than the CSI 300 benchmark. Further analyses validate the proposed components and show consistent improvements in both in-sample and out-of-sample performance throughout optimization. The code is available at https://github.com/transcend-0/TradeGrad.

</details>


### [262] [Keeping JEPA World Models Plannable When Little of the Frame Moves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03137)
*Florian Strohm,Patrick Wagner,Jannik Schwab,Marco Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 语言目标规划失败源于世界模型编码器对动作不敏感；加入逆动力学辅助损失可修复潜表征并大幅提升规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用语言而非目标帧来指定规划目标是与潜世界模型交互的自然接口，但这需要场景中存在多个可用语言区分的物体，而现有基准（如PushT）缺乏这种语言判别需求，因此无法有效测试该能力。

Method: 构建SLIM推物基准：同一场景中含多个小物体，配对的视觉与语言目标。以能解PushT的LeWM世界模型为基线，用探针定位失败原因；提出一种逆动力学辅助损失，作用于编码器潜变量，并通过测试时丢弃的共享头作用于预测潜变量。设计廉价的动作敏感性探针（无需环境访问）作为经验必要条件，并在修复后的潜空间上训练小型语言目标头，支持单句及分阶段句子目标。

Result: LeWM在SLIM上成功率低于1%，而使用模拟器状态的脚本控制器可解所有层级。探针显示编码器潜变量几乎对动作不敏感，无法解码推杆或物体位置，rollout 也不优于原样复制。加入逆动力学辅助损失后，所有探针均恢复，成功率从0.003升至0.35（难推层级0.16，目标无关策略为0），并在两倍训练时长下提升PushT。对照实验将修复归因于流入编码器的梯度。动作敏感性探针低于阈值时均无法规划。语言目标头在修复潜空间上导航达0.84（视觉目标oracle为1.00），能在干扰物替换时跟踪被命名区域，并对未见名词优雅退化。单句目标很少完成推物，但分阶段句子使中等与难推层级从0.04升至0.25，与目标帧oracle相当，且阶段切换也可仅从潜变量读出。

Conclusion: 潜世界模型的规划失败可归因于编码器潜表征的动作不敏感性，逆动力学辅助损失能有效修复该表征并提升规划与语言目标跟随能力；动作敏感性探针可作为无需环境访问的必要条件指标。

Abstract: Specifying a goal in language rather than as a goal frame is a natural interface for planning with a latent world model, but testing it needs scenes in which language must discriminate between several objects. We build SLIM, a pushing benchmark with several small objects and paired visual and language goals on identical scenes. On SLIM a LeWM world model that solves PushT succeeds on under 1% of trials, although a scripted controller with simulator state solves every tier. Probes locate the failure in the encoder: its latent is nearly action-insensitive, neither pusher nor object positions can be decoded from it, and rollouts are no better than copying the current latent forward. One inverse-dynamics auxiliary loss, applied to encoder latents and to predicted latents through a shared head discarded at test time, restores every probe and raises success from 0.003 to 0.35 (0.16 on the hard pushing tier, where a goal-agnostic policy scores zero), and improves PushT at twice the trained horizon. Controls attribute the repair to the gradient into the encoder, and a response sweep shows that the vanilla model plans once enough of the frame responds to actions. A cheap action-sensitivity probe, computable without environment access, acts as an empirical necessary condition: all configurations below its threshold failed to plan. On the repaired latent, a small language-goal head plans from sentences without retraining the world model: it reaches 0.84 on navigation (visual-goal oracle 1.00), follows the named zone when it is swapped with a decoy, and degrades gracefully to unseen nouns. A single goal sentence rarely completes a push, but given the push as a sequence of stage sentences the head raises success on the medium and hard pushing tiers from 0.04 to 0.25, on par with the goal-frame oracle, also when the switch between stages is read from the latent alone.

</details>


### [263] [Toward SLM-based agentic task-tool intent matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03213)
*Chiara Troiani,Arash Salarian,Majed El Helou,Benjamin Ryder,Jean Diaconu,Hervé Muyal,Marcelo Yannuzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨使用小型语言模型（SLM）作为任务-工具相关性分类器，对AI代理的每次工具调用进行独立验证，以弥补传统授权机制无法评估代理认知意图的不足，并通过提示优化、监督微调与GRPO强化学习对SLM进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着工具增强型AI代理系统横向扩展，工具调用交互数量激增，需要低延迟、可本地部署的自动化逐次调用监督。传统授权方案只能判断代理是否有权调用某工具，却无法评估其底层认知——即工具选择是否是满足任务意图的逻辑且相关的步骤。因此，即使调用被允许，仍可能偏离任务意图，甚至出现恶意代理偏离调用或诱导其他代理做出不符合任务意图的调用组合。故每次调用都需验证。

Method: 将小型语言模型（SLM）用作任务-工具相关性分类器，独立评估每次选定的工具是否与所分配任务相关，并向下游执行环节返回相关性信号。研究构建了一个新数据集，其中的多工具任务所需工具跨越不同的模型上下文协议（MCP）服务器，并采用提示优化、监督微调以及通过GRPO实现的强化学习来优化和特化SLM。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验结果或性能指标。

Conclusion: SLM可作为低延迟、可本地部署的任务-工具相关性分类器，为工具增强型AI代理的每次调用提供独立验证，弥补传统授权机制在评估代理认知意图方面的缺陷；研究通过提示优化、监督微调和GRPO强化学习对其进行优化与特化。

Abstract: Tool-equipped AI agents use tool calls to access data and act on external systems. Horizontal growth of agentic systems increases the number of these interactions, and further motivates the need for automated, per-call oversight that can operate at low latency and/or on-prem. Conventional authorization schemes can determine whether an agent is allowed to invoke a tool, but cannot assess the agent's underlying cognition, specifically, whether the tool selection represents a logical, relevant step toward satisfying the intent of the task or not. Consequently, an allowed call may still deviate from the task's intent: a rogue agent might deviate the calls or nudge other agents to make a combination of calls that would not align with the intent of the task. Therefore, every call needs to be verified. In this study we investigate the applicability of Small Language Models (SLMs) to this purpose: an SLM functions as a task-tool relevance classifier that evaluates every selected tool independently against the assigned task and returns a relevance signal for downstream enforcement. Equipped with a novel dataset with multi-tool tasks whose required tools span distinct Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers, we used prompt-optimization, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning through GRPO to optimize and specialize SLMs.

</details>


### [264] [Learning a Fact Is Not Learning How to Retrieve It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03251)
*Chaemin Jang,Jihee Kim,Dongman Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文区分"事实学习"与"事实检索"：先用不同请求形式（如"A的首都是B"与"A的首都："）进行第一阶段训练，再用相同陈述句训练新事实。结果发现，第一阶段接触多种请求形式的模型能更好地跨形式检索新事实，其关键机制是答案前的"上下文状态"（context state）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型对同一事实的不同请求形式（request forms）表现差异显著：训练时见过"A的首都是B"的形式，却在"A的首都："后无法输出B。作者希望将"学习事实"与"检索事实"解耦，探究检索能力是否可独立于具体事实而预先习得，以及其背后的内部表征机制。

Method: 采用两阶段训练设计。第一阶段（request-form training）：一个模型以五种请求形式看到每个事实，另一个模型仅以陈述句形式看到相同事实。第二阶段（target-fact training）：两模型以完全相同的方式（均为陈述句）学习新事实。随后比较二者在新事实上的检索表现。分析层面，考察答案前一刻的隐状态（context state），比较不同请求形式下的状态相似度；并通过在检索时或仅在学习时干预该状态，检验其因果作用。

Result: 两模型在新事实的陈述句检索上表现几乎相同，但在其他请求形式上差异显著，说明检索形式的能力可在事实学习之前获得。第一阶段见五种形式的模型，对同一事实的不同请求形式产生更相似的上下文状态。在检索时改变该状态可启用或阻止已学事实的检索，且该效应可跨事实及关系（如首都、货币）迁移。仅在目标事实训练阶段干预上下文状态，即可改变后续检索，无需测试时干预。

Conclusion: 后续检索取决于早期的请求形式经验以及事实学习时的上下文状态。事实的学习与检索可分离：模型可先学会"如何通过某种请求形式检索"，再学习事实本身；上下文状态是连接请求形式经验与事实检索的关键中介。

Abstract: A model trained on "The capital of X is Y" may produce "Y" after "The capital of X is" but fail after "The capital of X:". We call these different ways of eliciting the same fact request forms. To separate learning a fact from retrieving it, we train two models in two stages. In the first stage (request-form training), one model sees each fact in five forms and the other sees the same facts only as statements. In the second stage (target-fact training), both receive identical training on new facts, all as statements. Both then retrieve the new facts almost equally well from statements, but differ sharply on other request forms. Thus, a model can learn how to retrieve through a request form before it learns the facts. To understand this difference, we examine the hidden state immediately before the answer, which we call the context state. When given two different request forms for the same fact, the model trained on five forms in stage one produces more similar context states than the model trained on statements alone in that stage. Changing this state at retrieval time can enable or prevent retrieval of an already learned fact, and the same effect transfers across facts and factual relations, such as capitals and currencies. To test its role during learning, we change the context state only during target-fact training. This intervention changes later retrieval without intervention at test time. Together, these results show that later retrieval depends on earlier request-form experience and the context state during fact learning.

</details>


### [265] [EVOL: Simulator-Guided Evolutionary Expert Synthesis for Deployment-Free Learning Path Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03273)
*Geonwoo Bang,Dongho Kim,Moohong Min*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出EVOL框架，将机器人学中基于模拟器的示范学习范式引入学习路径推荐，用知识追踪模拟器通过进化搜索合成专家示范，并训练无部署依赖的蒸馏策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习用于学习路径推荐存在两个耦合障碍：一是策略需一次性承诺长度为L的概念序列，无中间反馈，导致组合搜索空间随L超指数增长且仅在终点给出奖励；二是稀疏奖励RL的自然解法是专家学习路径，但教育数据中不存在此类示范，学生日志只记录实际行为而非理想路径。

Method: 借鉴机器人基于模拟器的示范学习思路：用知识追踪模拟器通过进化搜索为每位学习者合成专家示范，同时训练一个免部署（deployment-free）策略，将示范蒸馏进前馈学习器。具体实现采用非对称actor-critic，actor在部署现实条件下进行盲规划并承诺完整序列，critic在训练时利用特权模拟器状态。

Result: 在ASSIST15、Junyi、EdNet三个数据集（39-189个概念）及L=5、10、20路径长度下，EVOL超越涵盖启发式、序列式、RL、图增强RL与LLM增强方法的8个基线。进一步比较BC、AWR、DAPG三种模仿策略，发现最终性能由进化专家质量主导，而非具体模仿目标。

Conclusion: 将基于模拟器的示范学习从机器人领域迁移至教育场景可有效缓解LPR中的稀疏奖励与组合搜索难题；性能瓶颈在于进化专家示范质量而非模仿算法选择，为无专家教育数据下的路径推荐提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for learning path recommendation (LPR) faces two coupled obstacles. First, the policy must commit to a sequence of L concepts without intermediate feedback, producing a combinatorial search space that grows super-exponentially with L and provides reward only at the final step. Second, expert learning paths would be the natural cure for sparse-reward RL, but they do not exist in educational data, because student logs record what learners did, not what they should have done. We address both obstacles by importing a recipe from simulator-based demonstration learning in robotics: the knowledge tracing simulator is used both to synthesize per-learner expert demonstrations through evolutionary search and to train a deployment-free policy that distills these demonstrations into a feed-forward learner. Our framework, EVOL, instantiates this pipeline with an asymmetric actor-critic where the actor commits to deployment-realistic blind planning while the critic exploits the privileged simulator state during training. Across three datasets (ASSIST15, Junyi, and EdNet; 39-189 concepts) and path lengths L = 5, 10, and 20, EVOL surpasses 8 baselines spanning heuristic, sequential, RL, graph-enhanced RL, and LLM-enhanced methods. We further compare three imitation strategies (BC, AWR, and DAPG) and show that final performance is governed by the quality of evolutionary experts rather than by the particular imitation objective.

</details>


### [266] [JOVE: Joint Execution and Verification for Resource-Aware LLM Task Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03296)
*Haoran Zhang,Dongjun Kim,Seohyeon Cha,Kevin S Chan,Ananthram Swami,Gustavo De Veciana,Haris Vikalo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JOVE是一个在线框架，通过联合分配异构LLM执行器并选择中间输出进行付费验证，在长期预算和单查询延迟约束下优化执行与学习的权衡，实现亚线性质量学习遗憾，在四个推理基准上成本与延迟降低至少3.17倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂推理查询可分解为有向无环任务图并分布到异构LLM上执行，以并行降低延迟并让较小模型解决复杂任务。但实践中LLM对特定子任务的适用性先验未知，仅凭执行无法判断输出正确性，因此需要引入验证并考虑执行与学习的长期权衡。

Method: JOVE将执行与验证决策建模为每查询混合整数线性规划（MILP）序列求解；通过在线学习基于验证反馈更新任务相关的LLM质量估计，并加入信息增益奖励项将学习价值纳入分配决策；在随机且初始未知的服务质量、调用成本和执行时间下，兼顾长期预算与单查询延迟约束。

Result: 在自然假设下，JOVE获得亚线性的质量学习遗憾（sublinear quality-learning regret）。在四个推理基准上，JOVE相对标准推理基线达到有竞争力的准确率，同时平均成本与延迟至少降低3.17倍。

Conclusion: JOVE表明，将中间输出付费验证与执行分配联合优化、并将学习价值显式纳入决策，可在预算与延迟约束下有效提升异构LLM编排的效率。

Abstract: Complex reasoning queries can be decomposed into directed acyclic task graphs and distributed across heterogeneous LLMs, reducing latency through parallelism and enabling smaller models to solve complex tasks. In practice, however, the suitability of an LLM for a given subtask may be a priori unknown, and execution alone does not reveal output correctness. We propose JOVE, an online framework that jointly assigns executor LLMs and selects intermediate outputs for paid verification. Verification runs asynchronously and is used to improve future allocations, so the system must balance spending on execution now against learning for later. We study how to optimize this trade-off under a long-term budget and a per-query latency constraint, with stochastic, initially unknown LLM service quality, invocation costs, and execution times. JOVE makes execution and verification decisions by solving a sequence of per-query mixed-integer linear programs. Online learning updates task-dependent estimates of LLM quality based on verification feedback, while an information-gain bonus incorporates the value of learning into allocation decisions. Under a natural set of assumptions, we establish sublinear quality-learning regret for JOVE. Across four reasoning benchmarks, JOVE achieves competitive accuracy against standard inference baselines while reducing average cost and latency by at least 3.17 times.

</details>


### [267] [Optimal Planning in a Dynamic World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03312)
*Devin Wild Thomas,Solomon Eyal Shimony,Wheeler Ruml,Erez Karpas,Shahaf S. Shperberg,Andrew Coles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"任意起始时间规划"（any-start-time planning）问题，用复合到达时间函数（cATF）紧凑编码最优计划随起始时间的变化，并证明其规模至多与问题规模成线性关系，在SIPP实验上优于依赖重规划的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中可行状态或动作随时间变化（如移动障碍物场景、列车仅在停站时可登车），最优计划及其时长依赖于执行开始时间。然而现实中开始时间往往在规划完成后才确定，而已有规划工作大多忽略动态性或假设开始时间已知，前者不适用、后者不切实际。

Method: 放松"开始时间已知"的假设，形式化定义 any-start-time planning 问题；提出复合到达时间函数（compound arrival time function, cATF）这一数据结构，将最优计划紧凑表示为起始时间的函数；给出基于启发式图搜索的通用规划算法，沿边传播函数而非标量代价来组装 cATF。

Result: 证明 cATF 的规模至多与问题规模成线性关系（size at most linear in the problem size）；针对 SIPP 的实现实验表明，在困难问题上依赖重规划的智能体常失败，而使用 cATF 的任意起始时间算法在起始时间确定后可快速查得最优计划。

Conclusion: 通过实现时变计划的高效表示与推理，该工作为动态世界中的规划奠定了基础。

Abstract: Background: We address the problem of planning when the set of feasible states or actions changes over time. For example, in the problem of path planning among moving obstacles (sometimes known as SIPP), the feasibility of being at a particular location can change as the obstacles move. Or, the action of boarding a particular train is feasible only while it is stopped at the station. This dynamism means that the optimal plan and its duration can change depending on when execution begins. In practice, execution start time is often unknown until planning has completed or another agent gives the go-ahead. However, most prior planning work either ignores dynamism or assumes a known start time. This makes it straightforward to assess state and action feasibility but is impractical for some applications. Objectives: In this paper, we relax the assumption of a known start time. We define the setting of {\em any-start-time planning} and provide algorithms for it. Methods: We present a data structure called a compound arrival time function (cATF) that compactly encodes the optimal plan as a function of start time. We provide general-purpose planning algorithms, based on heuristic graph search, that assemble cATFs by propagating functions along edges instead of scalar costs. Results: We prove that the size of a cATF is at most linear in the problem size. An experimental evaluation of an implementation for the specific problem of SIPP shows that, on difficult problems, agents that rely on replanning often fail, while any-start-time algorithms using cATFs can quickly look up the optimal plan once the execution start time is known. Conclusions: By enabling efficient representations and reasoning for time-dependent plans, this work provides a foundation for planning in dynamic worlds.

</details>


### [268] [Lightweight, Rubric-Guided Trajectory Evaluation for Production AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03315)
*Linh-An Phan,MingXue Wang,Guangyu Wu,Feng Pan,Zhaoyu Pang,Yanbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 LiteTrajEval，一种预算受限的轻量级轨迹评估架构：离线构建领域规则画像，在线预处理轨迹、标记启发式失败信号并按固定全局预算序列化，最后由单个评分引导的 LLM 评判器生成结构化诊断报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹评估对提升 LLM 智能体的可靠性至关重要，但生产环境中反复运行评测成本高昂；且现代智能体产生的长轨迹包含工具调用、观察、重试与外部输出，其中并非所有原始 token 对故障诊断同等有用。

Method: LiteTrajEval 采用离线和在线两阶段设计：离线导出紧凑的领域专用规则画像；在线对每条轨迹进行预处理，标记启发式失败信号，在固定全局预算下将其序列化，并调用单个 rubric 引导的 LLM 评判器，输出结构化诊断报告。

Result: 在公开的 Magentic-One 风格与 τ-bench 风格轨迹数据集上，LiteTrajEval 相对 AgentRx 将故障定位与人工标注的对齐度在 Magentic-One 上提升约 20–35 个百分点，在 τ-retail 上最高提升 23 个百分点，同时成本降低约 6 倍，评估时间减少 8 倍以上。

Conclusion: LiteTrajEval 在显著降低评估成本与时间的同时有效提升了轨迹故障定位的准确性，且已部署于企业级智能体平台中。

Abstract: Trajectory evaluation is essential for improving the reliability of LLM-based agents, but production use makes it expensive to run repeatedly. Modern agents generate long traces containing tool calls, observations, retries, and external outputs, while not all raw tokens are equally useful for diagnosis. We present \textit{LiteTrajEval}, a lightweight architecture for budget-bounded trajectory evaluation. LiteTrajEval derives compact domain-specific rule profiles offline, then preprocesses each trajectory online, marks heuristic failure signals, serializes it under a fixed global budget, and invokes a single rubric-guided LLM judge to produce structured diagnostic reports. Evaluated on public Magentic-One-style and $τ$-bench-style trajectory datasets, LiteTrajEval improves failure-localization alignment with human annotations by roughly 20--35 percentage points on Magentic-One and up to 23 percentage points on $τ$-retail compared with AgentRx, while reducing cost by about 6$\times$ and evaluation time by more than 8$\times$. This solution has also been deployed in our enterprise agentic platform.

</details>


### [269] [Multi-Task Evolution for Zero-Shot Cross-Problem Generalization using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03316)
*Zhouliang Xie,Changliang Zhou,Genghui Li,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MECo：基于LLM的多任务进化框架，通过任务条件化启发式种群、迁移差距引导的种群交互与组合覆盖选择，实现零样本跨问题启发式泛化，在32个VRP/FJSP变体上取得最低平均成本并优于八种AHD基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计有效的组合优化启发式需要大量专家经验与反复搜索。LLM虽可自动生成与精炼启发式，但启发式搜索通常依赖被优化问题本身的评估反馈，难以仅凭源任务反馈泛化到新问题定义，跨问题零样本泛化因此成为核心挑战。

Method: 提出MECo，LLM驱动的多任务进化框架：维护任务条件化（task-conditioned）的启发式种群；基于跨任务种群性能定义"迁移差距"（transfer gap）以引导种群间交互，实现启发式的迁移与重组；辅以选择准则，通过奖励每个成员对源任务组合的额外覆盖度来构建紧凑启发式集合；所得集合直接应用于目标任务，无需进一步搜索或自适应。

Result: 在车辆路径（VRP）与柔性作业车间调度（FJSP）的32个问题变体上，同等预算下MECo的平均成本低于八种自动化启发式设计（AHD）基线；在域外（OOD）问题上，于两个问题族中均优于各自最强基线；将MECo框架与不同AHD方法结合，也能提升其在两族内的ID与OOD性能。

Conclusion: MECo通过多任务进化与迁移引导的启发式复用，实现了有效的零样本跨问题启发式泛化，且其框架具备方法无关的通用性，可与多种AHD方法结合以提升性能。

Abstract: Designing effective heuristics for diverse combinatorial optimization problems requires substantial expertise and repeated search. Large language models (LLMs) automate heuristic generation and refinement, but heuristic search typically depends on evaluation feedback from the problem being optimized. Generalizing to new problem definitions using only source-task feedback therefore remains a central challenge. We introduce MECo, an LLM-driven multi-task evolutionary framework for zero-shot cross-problem generalization. MECo maintains task-conditioned heuristic populations and uses a transfer gap based on cross-task population performance to guide their interactions. These interactions enable the transfer and recombination of heuristics. A complementary selection criterion then constructs a compact heuristic set by rewarding each member's additional coverage of source combinations. The selected set is applied to target problems without further search or adaptation. Experiments on 32 problem variants across vehicle routing (VRP) and flexible job-shop scheduling (FJSP) show that MECo achieves the lowest mean costs compared with eight automated heuristic design (AHD) baselines under the same budgets. On out-of-domain problems, it outperforms the strongest baseline in each family. Moreover, integrating the framework of MECo with different AHD methods improves their ID and OOD performance in both families, supporting its effectiveness across different methods.

</details>


### [270] [Refinement Buys Intelligibility, Search Buys Identity: What Test-Time Compute Buys in Masked-Diffusion TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03320)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Ayush Pratap Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 扩散式TTS中,深度(参数)与精修步数(推理预算)并非可互换的计算资源:精修能弥合86.2%的可懂度差距,却仅弥合46.4%的说话人身份差距(1.86倍不对称),且该不对称在更长噪声调度下缩小但不消失;最佳-of-K搜索可弥补身份损失,剩余62%的身份缺陷源自编解码器而非生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
